/**
 * Lá»šP GIAO TIáº¾P API
 *
 * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T]
 * - Cáº­p nháº­t `FeMindmapDoc` Ä‘á»ƒ bao gá»“m cÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng cÃ i Ä‘áº·t global
 * (Ä‘Æ°á»£c tráº£ vá» tá»« `normalizeContentBEtoFE`).
 */

import api from './api';

// === CÃC IMPORT ===
import {
  // Types (Giao diá»‡n) cá»§a Backend
  BeMindmapDoc,
  BeMindmapContent,

  // HÃ m "PhiÃªn dá»‹ch"
  normalizeContentBEtoFE,
  normalizeContentFEtoBE,
  migrateOldGuestDataToBE, 
} from './dataMapper';
import {
  NodeData as FeNodeData,
  EdgeData as FeEdgeData,
} from '../app/store/useEditorStore';
// === Káº¾T THÃšC IMPORT ===

/**
 * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T] Äá»‹nh nghÄ©a cáº¥u trÃºc dá»¯ liá»‡u pháº³ng
 * mÃ  `Editor.tsx` mong Ä‘á»£i nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c tá»« hÃ m `get()`.
 */
export type FeMindmapDoc = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  ownerId: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  version: number;

  // === Ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c "lÃ m pháº³ng" ===
  nodes: FeNodeData[];
  edges: FeEdgeData[];
  layoutMode: string;
  theme: string;
  
  // [Má»šI] ThÃªm cÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng cÃ i Ä‘áº·t global
  fontFamily?: string;
  branchLineWidth?: number;
  isColoredBranch?: boolean;
  globalBranchColor?: string;
  backgroundColor?: string;
  activeColorThemeId?: string;
  
  // [Má»šI] ThÃªm relationships vÃ  summaries
  relationships?: any[];
  summaries?: any[];
};

/**
 * Äá»‹nh nghÄ©a cáº¥u trÃºc tÃ³m táº¯t
 */
export type MindmapSummaryDto = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
};

// =========================================================================
// mindmapsApi
// =========================================================================
export const mindmapsApi = {
  // ===== Core CRUD =====

  /**
   * Láº¥y danh sÃ¡ch tÃ³m táº¯t.
   */
  list: async () => (await api.get<MindmapSummaryDto[]>('/mindmaps')).data,

  /**
   * [Cáº¬P NHáº¬T] Láº¥y chi tiáº¿t 1 mindmap.
   */
  get: async (id: string): Promise<FeMindmapDoc> => {
    // 1. Gá»m API vÃ  nháº­n vá» dá»¯ liá»‡u chuáº©n BE (lá»“ng)
    const response = await api.get<BeMindmapDoc>(`/mindmaps/${id}`);
    const beDoc = response.data;

    // 2. "Dá»‹ch" trÆ°á»ng 'content' tá»« BE sang FE
    // feContent BÃ‚Y GIá»œ cÅ©ng chá»©a cÃ¡c cÃ i Ä‘áº·t global Ä‘Ã£ lÃ m pháº³ng
    const feContent = normalizeContentBEtoFE(beDoc.content);

    // 3. Tráº£ vá» má»™t object "pháº³ng" mÃ  Editor.tsx mong Ä‘á»£i
    return {
      id: beDoc.id,
      name: beDoc.name,
      ownerId: beDoc.ownerId,
      createdAt: beDoc.createdAt,
      updatedAt: beDoc.updatedAt,
      version: beDoc.version,

      // GÃ¡n content Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c dá»‹ch (bao gá»“m cáº£ cÃ i Ä‘áº·t global)
      ...feContent, 
    };
  },

  /**
   * Táº¡o má»›i 1 mindmap.
   */
  create: async (payload: {
    name: string;
    content: { nodes: FeNodeData[]; edges: FeEdgeData[] }; // Nháº­n List FE
  }) => {
    // 1. "Dá»‹ch" content FE sang BE (lá»“ng)
    // HÃ m nÃ y BÃ‚Y GIá»œ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng láº¥y cÃ i Ä‘áº·t global tá»« store
    const beContent = normalizeContentFEtoBE(
      payload.content.nodes,
      payload.content.edges
    );

    // 2. Gá»­i payload Ä‘Ã£ chuáº©n hÃ³a BE
    const response = await api.post<BeMindmapDoc>('/mindmaps', {
      name: payload.name,
      content: beContent,
    });

    return response.data; // Tráº£ vá» BeMindmapDoc
  },

  /**
   * Cáº­p nháº­t 1 mindmap (dÃ¹ng cho Auto-Save).
   */
  update: async (
    id: string,
    payload: {
      name: string;
      content: { 
        nodes: FeNodeData[]; 
        edges: FeEdgeData[];
        relationships?: any[];
        summaries?: any[];
      };
    }
  ) => {
    const feNodes: FeNodeData[] = payload.content.nodes || [];
    const feEdges: FeEdgeData[] = payload.content.edges || [];
    const feRelationships = payload.content.relationships || [];
    const feSummaries = payload.content.summaries || [];

    // 2. "Dá»‹ch" content FE (List) sang BE (List lá»“ng)
    // HÃ m nÃ y BÃ‚Y GIá»œ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng láº¥y cÃ i Ä‘áº·t global tá»« store
    const beContent = normalizeContentFEtoBE(feNodes, feEdges, feRelationships, feSummaries);

    // 3. Gá»­i payload Ä‘Ã£ chuáº©n hÃ³a BE (chá»‰ 'name' vÃ  'content')
    const response = await api.put<BeMindmapDoc>(`/mindmaps/${id}`, {
      name: payload.name,
      content: beContent,
    });

    return response.data; // Tráº£ vá» BeMindmapDoc
  },

  /**
   * XÃ³a 1 mindmap.
   */
  remove: async (id: string) => (await api.delete<void>(`/mindmaps/${id}`)).data,

  // ===== Aliases (CÃ¡c hÃ m tiá»‡n Ã­ch) =====

  /**
   * Cáº­p nháº­t tÃªn (DÃ¹ng cho Sidebar).
   */
  updateName: async (id: string, name: string) =>
    (await api.put<BeMindmapDoc>(`/mindmaps/${id}`, { name })).data,

  /**
   * Alias cho `remove`.
   */
  delete: async (id: string) => (await api.delete<void>(`/mindmaps/${id}`)).data,

  // ===== Guest -> Server Sync =====

  /**
   * Äá»“ng bá»™ Guest.
   */
  syncGuest: async (migratedDocs: BeMindmapDoc[]) =>
    (await api.post<MindmapSummaryDto[]>('/mindmaps/sync', migratedDocs)).data,

  /**
   * Táº¡o vÃ  Má»Ÿ.
   */
  createAndOpen: async () => {
    const feRootNode: FeNodeData = {
      id: 'root',
      nodeText: 'Chá»§ Ä‘á» chÃ­nh',
      x: 0,
      y: 0,
      shape: 'roundedRect',
      color: '#FFFFFF',
      textColor: '#4A5568',
      borderColor: '#CBD5E0',
      borderWidth: 2,
      borderStyle: 'solid',
      fontSize: 14,
      fontWeight: 'normal',
      fontStyle: 'normal',
      textDecoration: 'none',
      textAlign: 'CENTER',
      textCase: 'normal',
      nodeLength: 'fit',
      localStructure: 'default',
      branchLineStyle: 'bezier',
      branchLineEnd: 'none',
      branchLineThickness: 'normal',
      quickStyleId: 'default',
    };

    return mindmapsApi.create({
      name: 'Mindmap má»›i',
      content: {
        nodes: [feRootNode], // Gá»­i Máº¢NG FE
        edges: [],
      },
    });
  },
};

// Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i Hex sang HSL
export function hexToHSL(hex: string): { h: number; s: number; l: number } {
  let result = /^#?([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i.exec(hex);
  if (!result) return { h: 0, s: 0, l: 100 };

  let r = parseInt(result[1], 16) / 255;
  let g = parseInt(result[2], 16) / 255;
  let b = parseInt(result[3], 16) / 255;

  const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
  const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
  let h = 0, s = 0, l = (max + min) / 2;

  if (max !== min) {
    const d = max - min;
    s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
    switch (max) {
      case r: h = (g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0); break;
      case g: h = (b - r) / d + 2; break;
      case b: h = (r - g) / d + 4; break;
    }
    h /= 6;
  }

  return { h: Math.round(h * 360), s: Math.round(s * 100), l: Math.round(l * 100) };
}

// Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i HSL sang Hex
export function hslToHex(h: number, s: number, l: number): string {
  l /= 100;
  const a = s * Math.min(l, 1 - l) / 100;
  const f = (n: number) => {
    const k = (n + h / 30) % 12;
    const color = l - a * Math.max(Math.min(k - 3, 9 - k, 1), -1);
    return Math.round(255 * color).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  };
  return `#${f(0)}${f(8)}${f(4)}`;
}

//TÃ­nh toÃ¡n mÃ u ná»n dá»±a trÃªn Ä‘á»™ sÃ¢u (Depth-based Lightness).
// Sá»­ dá»¥ng ná»™i suy tuyáº¿n tÃ­nh (Lerp) Ä‘á»ƒ lÃ m nháº¡t mÃ u dáº§n Ä‘á»u.
export function getBranchColorByDepth(
  baseHex: string, 
  depth: number, 
  cutoffLevel: number = 3
): { bg: string; border: string } {
  // 1. Base Color (Gá»‘c)
  if (depth <= 1) {
    return { bg: baseHex, border: baseHex };
  }

  if (depth >= cutoffLevel) {
    return { bg: '#FFFFFF', border: baseHex };
  }

  const { h, s, l: startL } = hexToHSL(baseHex);
  const targetL = 96; 

  let t = (depth - 1) / (cutoffLevel - 1);
  if (depth === 2) {
    t = 0.4;
  }
  
  // CÃ´ng thá»©c Lerp cho Lightness: L_new = L_start + (L_target - L_start) * t
  const currentL = startL + (targetL - startL) * t;

  const fadedColor = hslToHex(h, s, currentL);
  
  // Vá»›i cÃ¡c node nháº¡t, viá»n nÃªn Ä‘áº­m hÆ¡n ná»n hoáº·c dÃ¹ng chÃ­nh mÃ u Ä‘Ã³
  return { bg: fadedColor, border: fadedColor };
}

 // TÃ­nh toÃ¡n Ä‘á»™ tÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n Ä‘á»ƒ chá»n mÃ u chá»¯ (Äen/Tráº¯ng) chuáº©n WCAG.
export function getContrastingTextColor(hex: string): '#000000' | '#FFFFFF' {
  const { r, g, b } = hexToRgbSimple(hex);
  // CÃ´ng thá»©c YIQ standard
  const yiq = ((r * 299) + (g * 587) + (b * 114)) / 1000;
  return (yiq >= 128) ? '#000000' : '#FFFFFF';
}

function hexToRgbSimple(hex: string) {
  const result = /^#?([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i.exec(hex);
  return result ? {
    r: parseInt(result[1], 16),
    g: parseInt(result[2], 16),
    b: parseInt(result[3], 16)
  } : { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 };
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(

/// <reference types="vite/client" />

interface ImportMetaEnv {
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_DOMAIN: string;
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_CLIENT_ID: string;
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_REDIRECT_URI: string;
  readonly VITE_LOGOUT_REDIRECT_URI: string;
  readonly VITE_API_URL: string;
  readonly VITE_COGNITO_USER_POOL_ID: string;
}

interface ImportMeta {
  readonly env: ImportMetaEnv;
}
